# Encoder 1321, world 3: decision 12

[Gallery index](index.md)

This gallery preserves all 552 scheduled first narrator positions and their 552 literal-template controls in the fixed export order across 12 archive cases. No favorable examples were selected. These are repeated reporting opportunities, not 552 independent worlds.

`A_only` is a hybrid record: copied W11 fields with the previous node replaced by a source current estimate. Conditional candidates inherit that record and replace the query node with a source forecast. Ordinary and analytic sources also model attention effects. Joined records additionally supply native W.

Narrator text is raw first narration, not certified faithful or conscious. Literal templates are deterministic controls. Incomplete, missing, invalid, or blank text remains explicitly unavailable or blank according to the unchanged `pipeline._report_text` helper; no partial response is promoted into a complete text. The exact returned text is HTML-escaped inside `<pre>` blocks. No prose-auditor or reference-rater outcomes are used.

## 01. s1321_w03_t12_A_A_only_baseline

Source: `A`; view: `A_only`; variant: `baseline`.

Narrator transport complete: `true`; availability: `retained_first_outcome`; API status: `completed`.

Narrator text SHA-256: `473467673b026588d4be9f1a37f26322cb57dc5953d1cba9d141051c56fdf16a`.

Narrator text:

<pre>Panel p0 holds 4×4 probabilistic location distributions for roles red and blue at nodes n0–n3. Current estimates (represented_time values in origins): n0 (copied prior record) — blue peaks at [0,1] (peak prob ≈0.999962), red at [0,3] (≈0.999707). n1 and n2 (copied priors) — both roles are uniform across all 16 cells (tie peaks). n3 (history estimate) — blue peaks at [1,2] (≈0.5358), red at [0,1] (≈0.3335). Candidate conditional records for commands k0, k1, k2 inherit the copied priors for n0–n2 (these are not new estimates). Only node n3 differs by command: under k0 blue peaks at [1,2] (≈0.8891), red at [0,1] (≈0.3847); under k1 n3 matches the current n3; under k2 blue peaks at [1,2] (≈0.3573), red at [0,1] (≈0.4966). Candidate command records are conditional estimates, not events that have occurred.</pre>

## 02. s1321_w03_t12_A_A_only_current_donor

Narrator text:

<pre>This packet presents fallible 4×4 location distributions for red and blue roles at nodes n0–n3 in panel p0. Current distributions (represented_time 12–13) are: n0 — blue peak at (0,1) with very high peak probability (~0.99996), red peak at (0,3) (~0.99971); n1 and n2 — uniform distributions across all 16 cells; n3 — blue peak at (1,2) (≈0.9996), red peak at (0,1) (≈0.994). Origins show many current entries were copied from a prior record (not new estimates); candidate records under commands k0, k1 and k2 are conditional estimates (not executed events). Across the three conditional command estimates, nodes n0–n2 match the copied/current peaks; node n3 shows the same peak cells for blue (1,2) and red (0,1) while peak probabilities differ slightly among k0, k1 and k2. No physical facts, object presence, subjective states or calibrated confidence beyond these distributional numbers are asserted.</pre>

## 04. s1321_w03_t12_A_A_only_prospective_donor

Source: `A`; view: `A_only`; variant: `prospective_donor`.

Narrator transport complete: `true`; availability: `retained_first_outcome`; API status: `completed`.

Narrator text SHA-256: `e34088cd623d9014465dd9ff8af2d022e516ac3b4e2d3ea17280e5535f001271`.

Narrator text:

<pre>This packet contains panel p0 distributions for red and blue over separate 4×4 local grids at nodes n0–n3. Current estimates (panel p0, view=current) come from copied prior records for n0–n2 (record_time=11, represented_time=12) and a history estimate for n3 (feedback_through=11, represented_time=12); copied prior records are not new estimates. Conditional candidate command records (k0,k1,k2, view=candidate) are command-conditioned estimates (represented_time=13 for inherited/command estimates) and are not events that have occurred. Current, and the candidate variants where provided, show: n0 — blue peaks at cell (0,1), red peaks at (0,3) in current and in each candidate; n1 and n2 — both roles are uniform (all 16 cells tied) in current and candidates; n3 — blue peaks at (1,2) and red peaks at (0,1) in current and in each candidate. Numeric peak probabilities and entropies are present in the packet but are reported here only as distributional summaries; conditional records differ slightly in their reported probabilities and entropies though their peak cells are the same where listed.</pre>

## 05. s1321_w03_t12_A_A_only_prospective_restored

Source: `A`; view: `A_only`; variant: `prospective_restored`.

Narrator transport complete: `true`; availability: `retained_first_outcome`; API status: `completed`.

Narrator text SHA-256: `a31b640712df17ddbf5bb1fd27b8d3f7bcb2b7580e70c2ca7abcef089210e28c`.

Narrator text:

<pre>Panel p0 holds fallible 4×4 location distributions for red and blue at nodes n0–n3. Current records (inherited or copied from record_time 11 or labeled history_estimate) are not new estimates; conditional command records (k0, k1, k2) are hypothetical branches, not executed events. Current: n0 is highly concentrated — blue peaks at (0,1) and red at (0,3). n1 and n2 are uniform across their 4×4 grids. n3 (current) shows a blue peak at (1,2) and a red peak at (0,1) with moderate probabilities. Across conditional commands the only substantive differences appear at n3: under k0 blue is strongly concentrated at (1,2) (much higher peak probability than current), under k1 blue remains the modal cell (1,2) but with a smaller peak, and under k2 the red peak at (0,1) grows while blue’s peak probability is reduced. Nodes n0, n1 and n2 are effectively unchanged between current and the three conditional command records.</pre>

## 08. s1321_w03_t12_A_joined_baseline

Source: `A`; view: `joined`; variant: `baseline`.

Narrator transport complete: `true`; availability: `retained_first_outcome`; API status: `completed`.

Narrator text SHA-256: `0ecd09f154716f84757b9a68affb7510ed9e2f6ec6a5678f2f799df863e91a7e`.

Narrator text:

<pre>Summary of supplied distributions (4×4 grid, rows increase downward, columns rightward). Panel p0 current: nodes n0–n2 are copied prior records (not new estimates); n0 shows blue peaked at (row0,col1) and red peaked at (0,3). n1 and n2 are uniform for both roles. n3 (history estimate) has blue peak at (1,2) and red peak at (0,1). Panel p0 conditional candidate records (k0,k1,k2) repeat the same peaks per node; for n3 all three candidate commands also peak blue at (1,2) and red at (0,1) though probabilities differ across candidates. Panel p1 current: n0–n2 mirror p0 patterns and are completed-record origins; n3 is uniform for both roles. Origins indicate copied prior records or conditional estimates; copied prior records are not new estimates, and conditional command records are conditional estimates, not executed actions. Current (panel view &quot;current&quot;) summaries: p0 — n0: blue peak at (0,1), red at (0,3); n1 and n2: both roles uniform over the 4x4 grid; n3: blue peak at (1,2), red at (0,1). p1 — n0: blue (0,1), red (0,3); n1 and n2 uniform; n3 uniform. Conditional command variants (candidate views k0,k1,k2) differ at n3: in p0 all candidates concentrate blue at (1,2) and red at (0,1) with small probability differences; in p1 candidates vary — k0 concentrates blue on cells (0,2),(0,3),(1,2),(1,3) and red on (0,0),(0,1),(1,0),(1,1); k1 leaves n3 uniform; k2 concentrates blue at (1,2) and red at (0,1). No physical facts or presences are inferred from these distributions.</pre>

## 11. s1321_w03_t12_A_joined_prospective_donor

Source: `A`; view: `joined`; variant: `prospective_donor`.

Narrator transport complete: `true`; availability: `retained_first_outcome`; API status: `completed`.

Narrator text SHA-256: `a2fd12483f6f71955e471227b3eeae5da9809d64d8ef9e1dd4629da654b8a9e1`.

Narrator text:

<pre>Summary: Panel p0 current (represented_time 12) contains inherited prior distributions for nodes n0–n2 and a history estimate for n3. Current p0: n0 — blue peaks at (0,1) (peak ~0.99996), red peaks at (0,3) (peak ~0.99971); n1 and n2 — both roles uniform over all 16 cells (tie peaks); n3 — blue peaks at (1,2) (peak ~0.99958), red peaks at (0,1) (peak ~0.99370). Panel p0 candidate records (k0,k1,k2) repeat the same n0–n2 distributions; their conditional estimates at n3 differ by command as supplied (these are conditional records, not executed events). Panel p1 current (completed record, represented_time 12) matches p0 for n0–n2 and is uniform at n3. Panel p1 candidates present alternative conditional estimates at n3: k0 concentrates blue on {(0,2),(0,3),(1,2),(1,3)} and red on {(0,0),(0,1),(1,0),(1,1)}; k1 is uniform at n3; k2 concentrates blue at (1,2) and red at (0,1). Copied prior records are not new estimates; conditional command records are not events that have already occurred.</pre>

## 12. s1321_w03_t12_A_joined_prospective_restored

Source: `A`; view: `joined`; variant: `prospective_restored`.

Narrator text SHA-256: `818ecd3c9e93ea09277aebfe3a5cd4c4d68a8c4a637f72ab1892c83eb1c69502`.

Narrator text:

<pre>This packet presents fallible location distributions for red and blue roles on 4×4 local grids, by panel and node. Panel p0 current (copied prior records—not new estimates): n0: blue peak at (0,1), red at (0,3). n1 and n2: uniform over all 16 cells. n3 (history estimate): blue peak at (1,2), red at (0,1). Panel p0 candidate-conditioned records (k0,k1,k2) repeat n0–n2 peaks; n3 peaks remain blue (1,2) and red (0,1) though peak probabilities differ across commands. Panel p1 current (completed records): n0–n2 match p0 current; n3 is uniform. Panel p1 includes native conditional records for n3 that differ by command: k0 yields four blue peaks (0,2),(0,3),(1,2),(1,3) and four red peaks (0,0),(0,1),(1,0),(1,1); k1 and k2 yield concentrated peaks as recorded. Conditional command records are alternative, not executed, branches.</pre>

Narrator text:

<pre>This packet contains fallible location distributions for red and blue roles at nodes n0–n3 in two panels (p0, p1). Current estimates (represented_time 13 or copied prior represented_time 12 as noted in origins) are given per panel. Panel p0 current: blue peaks at n0=[0,1] and n3=[1,2]; red peaks at n0=[0,3] and n3=[0,1]; nodes n1 and n2 are uniform for both roles. Panel p1 current: blue peaks on a 2×2 block at n3 (cells [0,2],[0,3],[1,2],[1,3]); red peaks on a 2×2 block at n3 (cells [0,0],[0,1],[1,0],[1,1]); n0 shows single-cell peaks and n1–n2 are uniform. Candidate command records (k0,k1,k2) are conditional estimates, not events that occurred; copied prior records are not new estimates. Differences across candidates are present mainly at panel p1 node n3: k0 matches the 2×2 block, k1 shows uniform peaks, and k2 concentrates at a single cell [1,2].</pre>

## 15. s1321_w03_t12_ordinary_A_only_baseline

Source: `ordinary`; view: `A_only`; variant: `baseline`.

Narrator transport complete: `true`; availability: `retained_first_outcome`; API status: `completed`.

Narrator text SHA-256: `71709e060007df956d994d9ed6a53c66cd35b5967e058f6246e98e7491fc5f8d`.

Narrator text:

<pre>Panel p0 contains fallible location distributions for red and blue at nodes n0–n3 on a 4×4 grid (rows increase downward, columns increase rightward). Current estimates (represented_time 12) include: n0 — blue concentrated at cell (row 0, column 1) (peak ≈0.99996), red concentrated at (0,3) (peak ≈0.99971); n1 and n2 — both roles uniform across all 16 cells; n3 — blue peaked at (1,2) (peak ≈0.8166), red peaked at (1,2) as well (peak ≈0.3287). Origins: some current cells are copied from a prior record (record_time 11) or are history estimates; copied prior records are not new estimates. Candidate conditional command records k0, k1, k2 are alternative, conditional estimates (feedback_through 11, represented_time 13) and are not events that have already occurred. Across commands most nodes (n0, n1, n2) keep the same peak patterns as current; node n3 differs by command: k0 shows both roles peaking at (1,2) with higher blue peak (≈0.9630) and moderate red peak (≈0.3546); k1 is similar to current; k2 places blue at (1,2) (≈0.8945) while red peaks at (0,1) (≈0.5017), changing relative horizontal/vertical relation reported in the data.</pre>

<pre>Panel p0 contains location distributions on 4×4 local grids for red and blue at nodes n0–n3. Current (represented_time 12) entries: n0 and n1–n2 are copied prior records (not new estimates); n0’s blue mass is concentrated at cell [0,1] (peak ≈0.99996) and red at [0,3] (peak ≈0.99971). n1 and n2 are uniform across all 16 cells for both roles. n3’s current history estimate (represented_time 12) has both blue and red peaked at [1,2] in this panel. Candidate conditional records (commands k0, k1, k2) are conditional estimates, not events. For k0 and k1, the per-node distributions match the current peaks at n0–n2 and both roles at n3 peak at [1,2]. For k2 the n3 candidate differs: blue peaks at [1,2] while red peaks at [0,1]. These statements describe the supplied distributions and the differences across conditional command records; origins and times are as supplied.</pre>

## 22. s1321_w03_t12_ordinary_joined_baseline

Source: `ordinary`; view: `joined`; variant: `baseline`.

Narrator text SHA-256: `4c804ef87f74a84f2c186ea16d69aa3c3683bee4fe06cbd755cb97dfa2db9f1d`.

Narrator text:

<pre>This packet contains two panels (p0, p1) of fallible 4×4 location distributions for red and blue at nodes n0–n3. Copied prior records in the packet are not new estimates; conditional command candidate records (k0,k1,k2) are conditional estimates, not executed events. Panel p0 current estimates: n0 — blue peak at (0,1), red at (0,3); n1 and n2 — uniform; n3 — both blue and red peak at (1,2) in the current view. In p0 candidate records n3 varies: k0 raises blue peak probability and slightly raises red there; k2 raises blue and shifts red peak to (0,1). Panel p1 current is uniform at n3; p1 candidates alter n3 differently: k0 concentrates blue mass in the top-right 2×2 and red in the top-left 2×2, k1 leaves n3 near-uniform, and k2 concentrates blue at (1,2) and red at (0,1). No physical presence or occurrence claims are made; these numbers describe only the supplied distributions.</pre>

Source: `ordinary`; view: `joined`; variant: `current_donor`.

Narrator transport complete: `true`; availability: `retained_first_outcome`; API status: `completed`.

Narrator text SHA-256: `f2201989f2e38c2b9ccc6ed57516ba510fc6406a58b1f5be021f5bafb1ac97fa`.

Narrator text:

<pre>This packet contains fallible 4x4 location distributions for red and blue at nodes n0–n3 in panels p0 and p1. Current estimates: p0 — n0: blue peak at cell [0,1], red at [0,3]; n1 and n2: both roles uniform across all 16 cells; n3: blue peak at [1,2], red at [0,1]. Origins labeled &quot;copied_prior_record&quot; or &quot;completed_record&quot; report earlier records (record_time 11 or 12) and are not new executed events. Candidate (command) records k0/k1/k2 are conditional estimates, not events that already occurred. Differences across command branches: in p0 candidate branches the n3 peaks remain on blue [1,2] and red [0,1] with minor numeric differences. In p1 candidate branches n3 varies: k0 concentrates blue on four upper-right cells and red on four upper-left cells; k1 gives uniform distributions; k2 concentrates blue at [1,2] and red at [0,1]. No physical or presence claims are made; numbers describe only the supplied distributions.</pre>

## 24. s1321_w03_t12_ordinary_joined_current_restored

Source: `ordinary`; view: `joined`; variant: `current_restored`.

Narrator transport complete: `true`; availability: `retained_first_outcome`; API status: `completed`.

Narrator text SHA-256: `12996d8500455317a6dee1331b492d64390c273a9f1488a7652e73073fe95eea`.

Narrator text:

<pre>Two panels (p0, p1) give fallible 4x4 location distributions for red and blue at nodes n0–n3 on the stated coordinate system. Current estimates: in both panels node n0 has a very strong blue peak at (0,1) and a very strong red peak at (0,3); nodes n1 and n2 are uniform across the grid; node n3 differs by panel: p0 current has both roles peaked at (1,2) with moderate-to-high peak probabilities, while p1 current is uniform at n3. Origins and timing are recorded with each distribution: copied prior records and inherited/current origins are not new estimates; conditional command records (candidate views labeled k0,k1,k2 and native_conditional records) are conditional estimates, not executed events. Across conditional commands the consistent facts are the strong peaks at n0 and the uniform n1/n2; node n3 varies by command and panel (candidate records show different peak locations and peak probabilities, e.g. some candidates place blue strongly at (1,2) while others spread probability or place red peaks elsewhere).</pre>

<pre>Using the supplied origins and times: copied prior records shown as current are not new estimates, and candidate (command) records are conditional alternatives, not executed events. Current panel p0: n0 shows a concentrated blue peak at (0,1) and red peak at (0,3); n1 and n2 are uniform 4×4 distributions for both roles; n3 shows a concentrated blue peak at (1,2) and red peak at (0,1). Candidate command records for p0 preserve those peaks at n0 and uniformity at n1/n2; n3’s conditional estimates across k0–k2 differ only in distribution sharpness but keep blue peak at (1,2) and red at (0,1). Current panel p1: n0 matches p0’s concentrated peaks; n1 and n2 are uniform; n3 in current p1 is uniform. Candidate records in p1 present distinct alternatives for n3: k0 concentrates blue on four cells [(0,2),(0,3),(1,2),(1,3)] and red on four cells [(0,0),(0,1),(1,0),(1,1)]; k1 leaves n3 uniform; k2 concentrates blue at (1,2) and red at (0,1).</pre>

Narrator text:

<pre>This packet contains two panels (p0, p1) of fallible 4x4 location distributions for red and blue at nodes n0–n3. Grids use rows increasing downward and columns increasing rightward. Copied prior records are not new estimates; conditional command records are hypothetical estimates, not executed events. Current (panel p0) summary: n0 — blue concentrated at (0,1) (peak ≈0.99996), red at (0,3) (peak ≈0.99971); n1 and n2 — uniform over all 16 cells; n3 — blue concentrated at (1,2) (peak ≈0.8166), red concentrated at (1,2) (peak ≈0.3287). Panel p1 current mirrors p0 for n0–n2; n3 current has blue concentrated on the four cells (0,2),(0,3),(1,2),(1,3) and red concentrated on the four cells (0,0),(0,1),(1,0),(1,1). Conditional command candidates differ: for p0 n3 the blue/red concentration and peak probabilities vary across k0/k1/k2; for p1 n3 k0,k1,k2 show different peak sets and magnitudes (k2 shifts blue to (1,2) and red to (0,1) with very large peak probabilities). No field measures or external facts are inferred; these are summaries of the supplied distributions only.</pre>

Source: `ordinary`; view: `joined`; variant: `W_restored`.

Narrator text SHA-256: `5044c535bc21d86186fa81226b9c31d9356dc95c4ec36ed42f00a2e71163502d`.

Narrator text:

<pre>This packet contains fallible location distributions for red and blue roles on 4×4 local grids at nodes n0–n3 in two panels (p0, p1). Current views (view=&quot;current&quot;) are reported with their origins and times: copied prior records are marked as such and are not new estimates; history or completed-record origins are represented at the supplied times; command-estimate and native conditional candidate records are conditional projections, not executed events. Current, panel p0: n0 concentrates blue at cell (0,1) and red at (0,3); n1 and n2 are uniform for both roles; n3 concentrates both roles at (1,2). Panel p1 current: n0 concentrates blue at (0,1) and red at (0,3); n1–n3 are uniform. Candidate command records (k0,k1,k2) provide alternate conditional distributions per panel/node; most nodes match the current peaks (e.g. p0 nodes n0–n2), while some conditional variants differ (notably p1 n3: k0 and k2 peak blue at (1,2) and red at (1,2 or 0,1) depending on command). Reported numeric peak locations and supplied derived summaries describe only the distributions, per the packet semantics.</pre>

## 29. s1321_w03_t12_analytic_A_only_baseline

Source: `analytic`; view: `A_only`; variant: `baseline`.

Narrator transport complete: `true`; availability: `retained_first_outcome`; API status: `completed`.

Narrator text SHA-256: `3e31fc12c7af4f3eec6440d109b6ddb13c62489faade9c42ce5789d592057cda`.

Narrator text:

<pre>Panel p0 gives fallible 4×4 location distributions for red and blue at nodes n0–n3. Current estimates (represented times cited in the packet) are: n0 — blue concentrated at cell (0,1) (peak probability ≈0.99996), red concentrated at (0,3) (peak ≈0.99971); n1, n2 and n3 — uniform 4×4 distributions (all cells equal). The packet notes that copied prior records are not new estimates and that conditional command records (candidate views k0–k2) are hypothetical conditionals, not events that have occurred. Conditional differences in p0: k0 and k1 leave n0–n2 like the current view but k0 alters n3 to four-cell peaks for each role (blue peaks at (0,2),(0,3),(1,2),(1,3); red peaks at (0,0),(0,1),(1,0),(1,1)). k2 changes only n3 from the current uniform pattern to strong peaks: blue at (1,2) and red at (0,1). Origins and represented times are carried with each distribution in the packet.</pre>

Narrator text:

<pre>Panel p0 gives fallible location distributions for red and blue at nodes n0–n3 on a 4×4 grid. Current estimates (copied prior records where indicated) show: n0 — blue sharply peaked at cell (0,1) and red sharply peaked at (0,3); n1 and n2 — both roles are uniform over all 16 cells; n3 — blue sharply peaked at (1,2) and red sharply peaked at (0,1). Conditional candidate records differ by command: under k0 and k2, nodes n0–n2 match the same sharp or uniform patterns but n3 is uniform under k0 and shows quadrant peaks under k2; under k1, n3 matches the current sharp peaks for blue and red. Copied prior records are not new estimates; candidate (command) records are conditional projections, not events that have occurred.</pre>

Narrator text:

<pre>Panel p0 distributions, using supplied origins and times. Copied-prior records are inherited estimates; candidate command records (k0,k1,k2) are conditional estimates, not executed events. Current (panel p0) at node n0: blue peaks at cell (0,1) (peak probability ~0.999962), red peaks at (0,3) (~0.999707). Current nodes n1 and n2: both red and blue are uniform over the 4×4 grid (0.0625 per cell, inherited from prior record). Current node n3 (history estimate) is uniform for both roles. Candidate differences appear only at n3: under k0 both roles show moderate multimodal concentrations (blue peaks at (0,2),(0,3),(1,2),(1,3); red peaks at (0,0),(0,1),(1,0),(1,1) with peak probabilities ~0.243 and ~0.245). Under k1 and k2 n3 shows strong concentration (blue peak at (1,2) ~0.999975; red peak at (0,1) ~0.998217). Other nodes under all commands match the current copied distributions. No physical, presence, subjective or calibrated-truth claims are made.</pre>

Narrator text:

<pre>Panel p0 gives fallible 4×4 location distributions for red and blue at nodes n0–n3. Current (view=current) estimates: n0 — blue peak at (0,1) (prob ≈0.99996), red peak at (0,3) (≈0.99971); n1 and n2 — both roles uniform over all 16 cells (each cell prob 0.0625); n3 — blue peak at (1,2) (≈0.99998), red peak at (0,1) (≈0.99822). Origins: current n0–n2 entries were copied from a prior record (record_time 11, represented_time 12) and are inherited into the current representation; current n3 is a history_estimate with feedback_through 11 (represented_time 12). Candidate command records k0, k1, k2 are conditional estimates (not executed events). Nodes n0–n2 show the same peaks or uniformity across all commands; node n3 differs by command: k0 shows uniform distributions, k1 matches the current peaks, and k2 concentrates blue on four top-right cells ((0,2),(0,3),(1,2),(1,3)) and red on four top-left cells ((0,0),(0,1),(1,0),(1,1)).</pre>

<pre>Panel p0 contains fallible 4-by-4 location distributions for red and blue at nodes n0–n3. Grids index rows downward (0–3) and columns rightward (0–3). Current (panel p0, view=current) estimates: n0 blue peaks at cell [0,1] (near-certain in the supplied distribution) and n0 red peaks at [0,3]; n1, n2 and n3 current distributions are uniform across all 16 cells. Origins in the packet mark the copied prior records as not new estimates and the n3 current entry as a history estimate (represented time fields supplied in the packet). Candidate conditional command records (k0, k1, k2) are conditional estimates, not executed events. Across candidates: n0 and n0 red distributions are consistent across commands (blue at [0,1], red at [0,3]); n1 and n2 remain uniform under all commands shown. Node n3 varies by command: under k0 the supplied distribution concentrates blue on cells [0,2],[0,3],[1,2],[1,3] and red on [0,0],[0,1],[1,0],[1,1]; k1 includes both a uniform alternative and a conditional alternative that concentrates blue at [1,2] and red at [0,1]; k2 concentrates blue at [1,2] and red at [0,1]. Copied-prior and conditional origins and represented times are those supplied in the packet; these characterizations describe only the supplied probability distributions.</pre>

Narrator text:

<pre>This packet gives fallible 4x4 location distributions for red and blue at nodes n0–n3 in two panels (p0, p1). Current estimates: in both panels n0 blue peaks at cell (0,1) and red peaks at (0,3) with very high peak probabilities; n1 and n2 are currently uniform across the 4×4 grid for both roles; p0 current n3 places blue mass on the upper-right 2×2 block ((0,2),(0,3),(1,2),(1,3)) and red mass on the upper-left 2×2 block ((0,0),(0,1),(1,0),(1,1)). Conditional candidate records differ only at node n3 in some commands: in both panels command k0 shows the same p0-style quadrant split at n3; command k1 leaves n3 uniform; command k2 concentrates p3-like peaks at (1,2) for blue and (0,1) for red in panel p1 (and in p0 k2 blue at (1,2), red at (0,1)). Copied or inherited origins are prior records; command-conditioned entries are conditional estimates, not executed events.</pre>

## 38. s1321_w03_t12_analytic_joined_current_restored

Source: `analytic`; view: `joined`; variant: `current_restored`.

Narrator transport complete: `true`; availability: `retained_first_outcome`; API status: `completed`.

Narrator text SHA-256: `b7895ef33ca8a531fde39904187e319982e3d94628b5a6944d246d0c394a7a48`.

Narrator text:

<pre>This packet reports fallible 4x4 location distributions for red and blue roles at nodes n0–n3 in two panels (p0, p1). Use origins/times as supplied: copied prior records are not new estimates; conditional command records are conditional estimates, not executed events. Current estimates (view=current): p0 n0 — blue peak at cell [0,1], red peak at [0,3]; p0 n1 and n2 — both roles uniform over all 16 cells; p0 n3 — blue peaked on cells [0,2],[0,3],[1,2],[1,3], red peaked on [0,0],[0,1],[1,0],[1,1]. Panel p1 current matches p0 for n0–n2; p1 n3 is uniform. Conditional command candidates mostly reproduce the current patterns for nodes n0–n2 across commands k0–k2. Notable command-specific differences occur for panel p1 node n3: candidate k0 predicts the four-cell top-right/left peaks (blue/red) like some command estimates, k1 leaves n3 uniform, and k2 concentrates blue at [1,2] and red at [0,1]. No propositions here assert physical facts beyond these supplied distributions.</pre>

## 40. s1321_w03_t12_analytic_joined_prospective_restored

Source: `analytic`; view: `joined`; variant: `prospective_restored`.

Narrator transport complete: `true`; availability: `retained_first_outcome`; API status: `completed`.

Narrator text SHA-256: `4f7e8b5ee90e450d3d4977ed8c4beeb8c512364d7d1d5a922465be1be7790a1a`.

Narrator text:

<pre>This packet contains paired red/blue location distributions over 4×4 local grids for nodes n0–n3 in panels p0 and p1. Copied prior records are not new estimates; conditional command records are conditional estimates, not executed events. Current estimates (panel p0 and p1): at n0 blue peaks at cell [0,1] and red at [0,3]; n1 and n2 show uniform distributions (all 16 cells tied); n3 in both panels is uniform in the current view. Conditional candidate records differ mainly at n3: some command branches (k0) allocate blue mass to the top-right 2×2 block and red mass to the top-left 2×2 block; other branches (k2) concentrate blue at [1,2] and red at [0,1]; k1 generally matches the current uniform or current n0/n1/n2 patterns. Numerical peak locations and which branches differ are provided in the proposition list.</pre>

## 42. s1321_w03_t12_analytic_joined_W_restored

Source: `analytic`; view: `joined`; variant: `W_restored`.

Narrator transport complete: `true`; availability: `retained_first_outcome`; API status: `completed`.

Narrator text SHA-256: `4e1b64f0429a0bf2837cff5bcc332791b70b93a8b3abda1775975c8121130174`.

Narrator text:

<pre>Using the supplied coordinate system and times: Panel p0 current estimates (copied prior records) show node n0 with a strong blue peak at cell (0,1) and a strong red peak at (0,3). In p0 current, nodes n1–n3 have both roles uniform across all 16 cells. Under p0 candidate commands: k0 changes n3 to four moderate blue peaks at (0,2),(0,3),(1,2),(1,3) and four moderate red peaks at (0,0),(0,1),(1,0),(1,1); k1 leaves n3 uniform; k2 makes n3 sharply peaked (blue at (1,2), red at (0,1)). Panel p1 current estimates match p0 for n0 (blue (0,1), red (0,3)) and have n1–n3 uniform. In p1 candidates, k0 alters n3 to the same moderate four-cell pattern as p0 k0, while k1 and k2 make n3 sharply peaked (blue at (1,2), red at (0,1)). Copied records are prior estimates; conditional command records are alternative estimates, not events that have occurred.</pre>

<pre>Panel p0 gives fallible 4×4 location distributions for red and blue at nodes n0–n3. Current records (archived_origin_time 13, represented_time 12, counterfactual_native_snapshot) show: n0 – blue concentrated at cell (row 0, col 1) (peak ≈0.99996) and red concentrated at (0,3) (peak ≈0.99971); n1 and n2 – both roles are uniform across the 4×4 grid; n3 – blue concentrated on four top-right cells (0,2),(0,3),(1,2),(1,3) (each ≈0.243) and red concentrated on four top-left cells (0,0),(0,1),(1,0),(1,1) (each ≈0.245). Conditional candidate records (native_conditional_record, represented_time 13) differ only at n3 across commands: under command k0 the candidate distribution at n3 matches the current concentration on those four cells; under k1 both roles at n3 are uniform; under k2 blue is tightly peaked at (1,2) and red at (0,1) (high peak probabilities). Nodes n0–n2 are the same across current and all candidate commands. Copied prior records are not new estimates, and conditional command records are not events that have already occurred.</pre>

## 45. s1321_w03_t12_W_W_only_W_restored

Source: `W`; view: `W_only`; variant: `W_restored`.

Narrator transport complete: `true`; availability: `retained_first_outcome`; API status: `completed`.

Narrator text SHA-256: `704591199d9b0f0fe26d2b69ce69c39ec44aca5cfd8f19450f17d6153bc882b7`.

Narrator text:

<pre>Panel p0 contains fallible 4x4 location distributions for red and blue at nodes n0–n3. Current (record_time 12, represented_time 12) summaries: n0 — blue peaks at cell (row 0,col 1) and red peaks at (0,3), both with very large peak probabilities; n1 and n2 — both roles uniform across all 16 cells; n3 — both roles uniform. Candidate conditional records (command k0/k1/k2; represented_time 13, native conditional records are not executed events): n0, n1, n2 candidates match the current summaries. n3 differs by command: under k0 blue mass concentrates on the two rightmost cells of the top two rows (peaks at (0,2),(0,3),(1,2),(1,3)); corresponding red mass concentrates on the two leftmost cells of those rows (peaks at (0,0),(0,1),(1,0),(1,1)). Under k1 and k2 n3 shifts to near-certain single-cell peaks: blue at (1,2) and red at (0,1). Copied prior records are presented as inherited current entries, not new conditional estimates; conditional command records are alternative conditional estimates, not events that have occurred.</pre>
